SCOPE AND METHOD
All 2178 tracked paths at the baseline commit are listed below; none were omitted.
Git modes/kinds and original blob identities came from git ls-tree. All original
blobs were read through nonexecuting git cat-file; each source/text blob was
UTF-8 decoded when possible. Every original .py was parsed with Python 3.12 AST;
two Python UTF-8 BOMs were handled as valid source encoding markers; every
.json and .ipynb was parsed with the standard JSON parser. JavaScript and
TypeScript import references were extracted conservatively, not typechecked by
this inventory procedure. Functional reference tests are reported separately.

Classification is inferred from directory responsibility, filename, manifest
schema and nonexecuting syntax/import analysis. It is NOT a claim that all
2,028,719 source/text lines received a manual semantic or security audit.
Deep review concentrated on runtime entrypoints, server modules, package and
workflow configuration, update/download/cloud helpers and trust boundaries;
see SECURITY_AUDIT.txt and FEATURE_INVENTORY.txt for those findings and limits.
Binary images/fonts/media were inventoried by size and blob identity, not
visually inspected or forensically analyzed. No original blob failed to read.
